crates/basilisk-canonical/src/binding.rs:82: /// Which statement in this module made a local binding.
crates/basilisk-canonical/src/binding.rs:83: ///
crates/basilisk-canonical/src/binding.rs:84: /// The payload is what lets a class hierarchy be keyed on DEFINITION SITE
crates/basilisk-canonical/src/binding.rs:85: /// rather than on a rendered name. Two classes spelled the same are two
crates/basilisk-canonical/src/binding.rs:86: /// definitions and never collide; one class reached through several names is
crates/basilisk-canonical/src/binding.rs:87: /// one definition and never splits.
crates/basilisk-canonical/src/binding.rs:88: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]
crates/basilisk-canonical/src/binding.rs:89: enum LocalBinding {
crates/basilisk-canonical/src/binding.rs:90:     /// A `class` statement. The range is the class's NAME token — the identity
crates/basilisk-canonical/src/binding.rs:91:     /// every consumer keys on.
crates/basilisk-canonical/src/binding.rs:92:     Class(TextRange),
crates/basilisk-canonical/src/binding.rs:93:     /// A `def` statement. The range is the function's NAME token, matching
crates/basilisk-canonical/src/binding.rs:94:     /// `FunctionInfo::name_span`. Several `def`s may bind one name — an
crates/basilisk-canonical/src/binding.rs:95:     /// `@overload` group, or a conditional redefinition — and each is its own
crates/basilisk-canonical/src/binding.rs:96:     /// definition; the positional rule picks the one in force at a use site.
crates/basilisk-canonical/src/binding.rs:97:     Function(TextRange),
crates/basilisk-canonical/src/binding.rs:98:     /// `name = <other name>`: an assignment that rebinds one name to whatever
crates/basilisk-canonical/src/binding.rs:99:     /// another name refers to at that point. Following it is the only way
crates/basilisk-canonical/src/binding.rs:100:     /// `Alias = Movie` / `class Film(Alias)` reaches `Movie`'s definition.
crates/basilisk-canonical/src/binding.rs:101:     AliasOf {
crates/basilisk-canonical/src/binding.rs:102:         /// The bare name on the right-hand side.
crates/basilisk-canonical/src/binding.rs:103:         name: String,
crates/basilisk-canonical/src/binding.rs:104:         /// Where that name is used, so it resolves under the same positional
crates/basilisk-canonical/src/binding.rs:105:         /// rule as any other use.
crates/basilisk-canonical/src/binding.rs:106:         offset: TextSize,
crates/basilisk-canonical/src/binding.rs:107:     },
crates/basilisk-canonical/src/binding.rs:108:     /// `name = <expression>`: an assignment whose right-hand side is not a
crates/basilisk-canonical/src/binding.rs:109:     /// bare name. The range is that EXPRESSION, which is the value's identity
crates/basilisk-canonical/src/binding.rs:110:     /// in this module — `T = TypeVar("T")` binds the `TypeVar(...)` call, and
crates/basilisk-canonical/src/binding.rs:111:     /// two assignments of the same-looking call are two distinct values.
crates/basilisk-canonical/src/binding.rs:112:     Value(TextRange),
crates/basilisk-canonical/src/binding.rs:113:     /// Any other binding statement — `def`, a loop target, an `except … as`
crates/basilisk-canonical/src/binding.rs:114:     /// name, a relative import.
crates/basilisk-canonical/src/binding.rs:115:     Other,
crates/basilisk-canonical/src/binding.rs:116: }
crates/basilisk-canonical/src/binding.rs:117: 
crates/basilisk-canonical/src/binding.rs:118: /// Alias hops followed before giving up.
crates/basilisk-canonical/src/binding.rs:119: ///
crates/basilisk-canonical/src/binding.rs:120: /// `A = B; B = A` is legal Python that binds both names to whatever they held

crates/basilisk-canonical/src/binding.rs:649:     ///
crates/basilisk-canonical/src/binding.rs:650:     /// Only the FIRST lookup is deferred, and only because the name being

crates/basilisk-checker/src/imports/builtins.rs:57: ///
crates/basilisk-checker/src/imports/builtins.rs:58: /// The BUILTIN SCOPE, read from the active typeshed generation. This is what a

crates/basilisk-checker/src/imports/builtins.rs:61: /// rather than about how a name in the file under analysis is written.
crates/basilisk-checker/src/imports/builtins.rs:62: ///
crates/basilisk-checker/src/imports/builtins.rs:63: /// `None` when the generation could not be read or parsed. A FAILURE IS NEVER

crates/basilisk-checker/src/imports/builtins.rs:66: /// can mistake "could not read `builtins.pyi`" for "`builtins.pyi` binds

crates/basilisk-checker/src/imports/builtins.rs:69: ///
crates/basilisk-checker/src/imports/builtins.rs:70: /// Parsed once per `(snapshot, target)` and shared by `Arc`. Unlike the class

crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/constructors_call_type/helpers.rs:109:     // signature's. Single inheritance — where the orders coincide — is the
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/constructors_call_type/helpers.rs:110:     // only shape this is right for by construction.
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/constructors_call_type/helpers.rs:111:     //
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/constructors_call_type/helpers.rs:112:     // The fall-through is also unsound in the other direction: reaching the

crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/enums_behaviors.rs:93: 
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/enums_behaviors.rs:94:         for class in &module.classes {
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/enums_behaviors.rs:95:             for base in &class.resolved_bases {

crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/enums_behaviors.rs:102:                     continue;
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/enums_behaviors.rs:103:                 };
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/enums_behaviors.rs:104:                 // `is_enum` is the transitive answer, closed over resolved

crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/enums_behaviors.rs:108:                 }

crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/enums_behaviors.rs:115:                     base.span,

crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/enums_behaviors.rs:120:                         base_class.name

crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/enums_behaviors.rs:129:             }
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/enums_behaviors.rs:130:         }
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:2: //! `generics_basic_2`: a non-type-variable argument in `Generic[...]` or
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:3: //! `Protocol[...]`.
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:5: //! [PEP 484](https://peps.python.org/pep-0484/#generics) requires every
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:6: //! argument of `Generic[...]` to be a type variable, and
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:7: //! [PEP 544](https://peps.python.org/pep-0544/#generic-protocols) says the same
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:8: //! of `Protocol[...]`. A concrete type in that position is an error:
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:11: //! class Bad1(Generic[int]): ...      # E — `int` is not a type variable
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:12: //! class Bad2(Protocol[int]): ...     # E — `int` is not a type variable
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:14: //!
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:15: //! # How the verdict is reached
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:16: //!
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:17: //! REBUILT. The deleted body read `ClassInfo::base_expression_names`, a
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:18: //! `Vec<String>` of RENDERED simple names, and matched them against a set of
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:19: //! type-variable names harvested the same way. That made the diagnostic a
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:20: //! function of spelling in both directions — an aliased type variable was
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:21: //! invisible, and an unrelated class spelled like one was accepted.
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:22: //!
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:23: //! Almost nothing here reads a name, and the one place that does is called out
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:24: //! below rather than hidden. Each piece of the judgment is a resolution:
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:25: //!
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:26: //! * **Is this base `Generic`/`Protocol`?** — the subscript's head expression
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:27: //!   is resolved through the module's binding table to a [`TypingForm`], so
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:28: //!   `from typing import Generic as G`, `typing.Generic`, and
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:29: //!   `typing_extensions.Protocol` all count, and a local `class Generic` does
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:30: //!   not.
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:31: //! * **Is this argument a type variable?** — two ways, in the order Python
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:32: //!   binds them. A legacy `T = TypeVar("T")` is reached by following the
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:33: //!   name's binding to the EXPRESSION it was assigned, and asking whether that
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:34: //!   expression calls a type-parameter factory; that is definition-site
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:35: //!   identity and holds through any number of aliases. `*Ts` and `Unpack[Ts]`
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:36: //!   ([PEP 646](https://peps.python.org/pep-0646/)) unwrap to the same
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:37: //!   question.
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:38: //!
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:39: //!   A [PEP 695](https://peps.python.org/pep-0695/) type parameter is the
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:40: //!   exception: it is matched by comparing the argument's spelling against the
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:41: //!   names in the class's own `type_params` list. That IS a string comparison.
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:42: //!   It is sound only because of how narrow it is — the type-parameter scope
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:43: //!   is opened by the `class` statement itself and closed at the end of the
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:44: //!   base list, it binds exactly the names in that list, and nothing can
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:45: //!   shadow or rebind them in between — so name equality within it is the
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:46: //!   scope lookup rather than a guess about what a spelling might mean. It is
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:47: //!   still the weakest link here, and it would become wrong the moment this
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:48: //!   comparison were reused anywhere the scope is not that small.
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:49: //!
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:50: //!   The lawful replacement is a scoped binding environment that resolves a
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:51: //!   PEP 695 parameter use to its declaration NODE. That does not exist yet.
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:52: //! * **Is this argument provably NOT one?** — it resolves to a class this
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:53: //!   module defines, to a canonical form that is not a type-parameter factory,
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:54: //!   or it is a literal. Anything else is UNKNOWN and reports nothing: an
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:55: //!   argument imported from a module this checker cannot see may well be a
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:56: //!   type variable ([CHKARCH-CONFORMANCE-MODE]).
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:58: use std::collections::HashSet;
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:59: 
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:60: use basilisk_resolver::{BindingTable, ResolvedModule, TypingForm};

crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:62: use ruff_text_size::{Ranged, TextRange};

crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:89: /// What an argument inside `Generic[...]` / `Protocol[...]` was resolved to be.
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:90: ///
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:91: /// Three-valued on purpose. The rule reports on [`Self::NotATypeVariable`] and
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:92: /// on nothing else, so an argument this module cannot resolve produces silence

crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:95: enum Argument {

crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:104: 
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:105: /// Emits `generics_basic_2` when a non-type-variable appears in `Generic[...]`
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:106: /// or `Protocol[...]`.

crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:129: /// Every class statement in the module, including those nested in functions

crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:132:     for stmt in body {
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:133:         match stmt {

crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:148:             }

crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:152:             }
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:153:             Stmt::With(stmt) => collect_class_defs(&stmt.body, out),
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:154:             Stmt::Try(stmt) => {

crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:166:                 }
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:167:             }
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:168:             _ => {}

crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:173: /// Check one class's `Generic[...]` / `Protocol[...]` bases.
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:174: fn check_class(
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:175:     class: &StmtClassDef,
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:176:     bindings: &BindingTable,
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:177:     factories: &HashSet<TextRange>,
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:178:     path: &str,
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:179:     diagnostics: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>,
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:180: ) {
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:181:     let Some(arguments) = class.arguments.as_deref() else {
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:182:         return;
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:183:     };
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:184:     // The one string comparison in this rule; see the module banner. A PEP 695
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:185:     // type-parameter list opens a scope that immediately encloses the base
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:186:     // list and binds exactly the parameters it declares, so within that scope
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:187:     // name equality is the lookup. Collected as `&str` because there is no
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:188:     // scoped binding environment to resolve the use site against yet.
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:189:     let type_params: Vec<&str> = class
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:190:         .type_params
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:191:         .iter()
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:192:         .flat_map(|list| list.iter())
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:193:         .map(|param| match param {
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:194:             TypeParam::TypeVar(param) => param.name.as_str(),
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:195:             TypeParam::TypeVarTuple(param) => param.name.as_str(),
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:196:             TypeParam::ParamSpec(param) => param.name.as_str(),
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:197:         })
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:198:         .collect();
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:199: 
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:200:     for base in arguments.args.iter() {
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:201:         let Expr::Subscript(subscript) = base else {
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:202:             continue;
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:203:         };
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:204:         if !matches!(
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:205:             bindings.form_of(&subscript.value),
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:206:             Some(TypingForm::Generic | TypingForm::Protocol)
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:207:         ) {
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:208:             continue;
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:209:         }
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:210:         for argument in basilisk_parser::subscript_elements(subscript) {
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:211:             if classify(argument, bindings, factories, &type_params) != Argument::NotATypeVariable {
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:212:                 continue;

crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:223: /// What one argument of `Generic[...]` / `Protocol[...]` denotes.
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:224: fn classify(
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:225:     expr: &Expr,
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:226:     bindings: &BindingTable,
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:227:     factories: &HashSet<TextRange>,
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:228:     type_params: &[&str],
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:229: ) -> Argument {
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:230:     // PEP 646 spells an unpacked `TypeVarTuple` two ways, and both ask the
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:231:     // same question of the operand.
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:232:     if let Some(inner) = unpacked_operand(expr, bindings) {

crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:234:     }
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:235:     if let Expr::Name(name) = expr {
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:236:         // The PEP 695 scope binds this name before any outer one can.

crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:238:             return Argument::TypeVariable;
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:239:         }
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:240:     }
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:241:     // A name bound by an assignment: follow it to the EXPRESSION it was bound
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:242:     // to — through any number of aliases — and ask whether that expression
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:243:     // constructed a type parameter. Two `TypeVar`s spelled alike are two
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:244:     // ranges here, and one `TypeVar` under three names is one range.
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:245:     if let Some(value) = bindings.local_value_binding(expr) {
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:246:         return if factories.contains(&value) {
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:247:             Argument::TypeVariable
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:248:         } else {
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:249:             Argument::NotATypeVariable
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:250:         };
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:251:     }
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:252:     // A class this module defines is never a type variable.
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:253:     if bindings.local_class_definition(expr).is_some() {
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:254:         return Argument::NotATypeVariable;
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:256:     // A symbol the canonical registry describes: `int`, `str`, `typing.Any`.
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:257:     // The type-parameter factories themselves are excluded because naming the
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:258:     // factory rather than an instance of it is a different diagnostic.
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:259:     if let Some(form) = bindings.form_of_with_builtins(expr) {

crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:262:         } else {
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:263:             Argument::NotATypeVariable
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:264:         };
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:265:     }
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:266:     match expr {
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:267:         // A literal in a type-parameter position cannot be a type variable

crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:275:         | Expr::Set(_)

crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:277:         // Everything else — an import, a dotted path outside the registry, a
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:278:         // string forward reference — is unresolved from here, and an
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:279:         // unresolved argument is not evidence of an error.
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:280:         _ => Argument::Unknown,
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:281:     }
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:282: }
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:283: 
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:284: /// The operand of a PEP 646 unpack, written either way.
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:285: ///
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:286: /// `*Ts` is syntax; `Unpack[Ts]` is a subscript of a registry symbol, resolved
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:287: /// through the binding table so an aliased import unwraps identically.
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:288: fn unpacked_operand<'e>(expr: &'e Expr, bindings: &BindingTable) -> Option<&'e Expr> {
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:289:     match expr {
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:290:         Expr::Starred(starred) => Some(starred.value.as_ref()),
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:291:         Expr::Subscript(subscript)

crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:296:         _ => None,
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:297:     }
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:298: }
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:299: 
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:300: /// The range of every expression in this module that CONSTRUCTS a type
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:301: /// parameter — `TypeVar(...)`, `TypeVarTuple(...)`, `ParamSpec(...)`.
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:302: ///
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:303: /// A range, not a name: it is the identity `BindingTable::local_value_binding`
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:304: /// hands back for any name bound to that construction, however many aliases
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:305: /// away.
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:306: fn type_parameter_constructions(bindings: &BindingTable, body: &[Stmt]) -> HashSet<TextRange> {
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:307:     body.iter()

crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:312:         })
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/generics_basic_2.rs:313:         .filter(|value| {

crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/shared/class_walks.rs:46: ///
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/shared/class_walks.rs:47: /// Each edge comes from a base expression the resolver already resolved

crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/shared/class_walks.rs:53: ///

crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/shared/class_walks.rs:66: /// Every method definition in the module, indexed by the DEFINITION SITE of
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/shared/class_walks.rs:67: /// its owning class and its own name.
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/shared/class_walks.rs:68: ///
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/shared/class_walks.rs:69: /// REBUILT from a `HashMap<(&str, &str), _>` keyed on the owning class's
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/shared/class_walks.rs:70: /// rendered name, which merged two classes declared with the same name in one
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/shared/class_walks.rs:71: /// module into a single entry — so a call to one class's method was checked
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/shared/class_walks.rs:72: /// against the other class's signature. `FunctionInfo::class_site` is the
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/shared/class_walks.rs:73: /// owning `class` statement's name span, the same key [`ClassGraph`] uses, so
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/shared/class_walks.rs:74: /// an index built here lines up with the hierarchy exactly.
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/shared/class_walks.rs:75: ///
crates/basilisk-checker/src/rules/shared/class_walks.rs:76: /// The method's own name is the name it is DEFINED under in the class body,

crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:1: //! Implements [RESOLV-CANONICAL-BINDING] and the transitive-recognition
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:2: //! foundation of [CHKARCH-DIAG-TYPEDDICT-READONLY-INHERITANCE]. See
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:3: //! docs/specs/CHECKER-ARCHITECTURE-SPEC.md#RESOLV-CANONICAL-BINDING
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:4: //! A module's class hierarchy, keyed on DEFINITION SITE.
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:5: //!
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:6: //! This is the rebuilt replacement for the deleted `class_by_name` /
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:7: //! `walk_bases` pair. The difference is the key. That walk built
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:8: //! `HashMap<&str, &ClassInfo>` from `ClassInfo::name` and looked up
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:9: //! `ClassInfo::bases`, a `Vec<String>` of simple names, so:
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:10: //!
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:11: //! * `Alias = Movie; class Film(Alias)` recorded the base as `"Alias"`, found
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:12: //!   no class of that name, and lost the inheritance edge entirely;
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:13: //! * `import other; class Film(other.Movie)` recorded the base as `"Movie"`
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:14: //!   and attached `Film` to whatever local class happened to be spelled that
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:15: //!   way — inventing a hierarchy the program does not have.
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:16: //!
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:17: //! Here the edges come from [`ClassInfo::resolved_bases`], which the visitor
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:18: //! fills by resolving each base expression through the module's binding table
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:19: //! down to the `class` statement it denotes. Two classes spelled the same are
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:20: //! two nodes; one class reached through several names is one node.
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:21: //!
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:22: //! [`ClassInfo::is_typed_dict`] is only set when a class names `TypedDict`
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:23: //! among its OWN bases. A subclass of another `TypedDict` is still a
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:24: //! `TypedDict`, so membership questions must walk the chain — that is what
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:25: //! [`ClassGraph::is_typed_dict`] is for.
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:26: 
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:27: use std::collections::{HashMap, HashSet};
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:28: 
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:29: use super::class_types::{ClassInfo, ResolvedBase};
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:30: use super::span::Span;
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:31: 
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:32: /// The result of walking a class's base chain: what was reached, and whether
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:33: /// anything was left unreachable.
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:34: ///
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:35: /// The two fields answer different questions and callers need both. A rule
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:36: /// asking "does this chain contain a `TypedDict`?" is satisfied the moment it
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:37: /// finds one, and [`Self::classes`] alone will do. A rule asking "is this
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:38: /// class NOT a subclass of that one?" is making a claim about the WHOLE
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:39: /// hierarchy, and may only make it when [`Self::complete`] is `true` —
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:40: /// otherwise the honest answer is "unknown" ([CHKARCH-CONFORMANCE-MODE]).
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:41: #[derive(Debug)]
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:42: pub struct Ancestry<'a> {
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:43:     /// The class and every ancestor of it defined in this module, most-derived
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:44:     /// first, each appearing exactly once.
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:45:     pub classes: Vec<&'a ClassInfo>,
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:46:     /// Whether every base of every class reached was resolved.
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:47:     ///
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:48:     /// `false` when some base is a class from another module, a name this
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:49:     /// module never bound, or anything else the binding table could not follow
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:50:     /// — so [`Self::classes`] is a LOWER BOUND on the real ancestry rather
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:51:     /// than the whole of it.
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:52:     pub complete: bool,
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:53: }
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:54: 
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:55: /// A module's classes, indexed by the definition site each one is keyed on.
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:56: ///
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:57: /// Borrows the class slice; building one is a single pass and costs no clones.
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:58: #[derive(Debug)]
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:59: pub struct ClassGraph<'a> {
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:60:     /// The classes this graph indexes, in declaration order.
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:61:     classes: &'a [ClassInfo],
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:62:     /// Definition site → index into [`Self::classes`].
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:63:     by_site: HashMap<Span, usize>,
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:64: }
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:65: 
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:66: impl<'a> ClassGraph<'a> {
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:67:     /// Index a module's classes by definition site.
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:68:     #[must_use]
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:69:     pub fn new(classes: &'a [ClassInfo]) -> Self {
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:70:         let by_site = classes
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:71:             .iter()
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:72:             .enumerate()
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:73:             .map(|(index, class)| (class.name_span, index))
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:74:             .collect();
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:75:         Self { classes, by_site }
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:76:     }
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:77: 
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:78:     /// The classes this graph indexes, in declaration order.
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:79:     #[must_use]
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:80:     pub fn classes(&self) -> &'a [ClassInfo] {
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:81:         self.classes
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:82:     }
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:83: 
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:84:     /// The class defined at `site`, if this module defines one there.
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:85:     #[must_use]
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:86:     pub fn at(&self, site: Span) -> Option<&'a ClassInfo> {
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:87:         self.classes.get(*self.by_site.get(&site)?)
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:88:     }
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:89: 
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:90:     /// `class` and every ancestor of it defined in this module, most-derived
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:91:     /// first, each visited exactly once — plus whether any edge was left
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:92:     /// unfollowed.
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:93:     ///
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:94:     /// Iterative by construction, so a chain of any depth costs heap rather
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:95:     /// than stack; the visited set bounds the walk to one visit per class, so
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:96:     /// a cyclic or self-referential base list — illegal Python, but reachable
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:97:     /// input (GitHub #398) — terminates in linear time instead of branching
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:98:     /// exponentially.
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:99:     ///
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:100:     /// A base that resolves outside this module contributes no edge, and that
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:101:     /// is exactly why the walk reports [`Ancestry::complete`]. Dropping such a
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:102:     /// base silently would hand callers a truncated chain indistinguishable
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:103:     /// from a fully known one, and the first thing a caller does with a chain
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:104:     /// is read a MISSING class out of it. `object` and the other typing forms
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:105:     /// are resolved, not unknown: they add no local node, but nothing about
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:106:     /// them is hidden.
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:107:     #[must_use]
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:108:     pub fn ancestry(&self, class: &'a ClassInfo) -> Ancestry<'a> {
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:109:         let mut visited: HashSet<Span> = HashSet::new();
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:110:         let mut order: Vec<&'a ClassInfo> = Vec::new();
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:111:         let mut worklist: Vec<&'a ClassInfo> = vec![class];
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:112:         let mut complete = true;
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:113:         while let Some(current) = worklist.pop() {
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:114:             if !visited.insert(current.name_span) {
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:115:                 continue;
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:116:             }
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:117:             order.push(current);
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:118:             // Reversed so the worklist pops bases in declaration order, which
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:119:             // is the order a subclass's fields shadow inherited ones.
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:120:             for base in current.resolved_bases.iter().rev() {
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:121:                 match base.resolved {
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:122:                     ResolvedBase::LocalClass(site) => match self.at(site) {
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:123:                         Some(base_class) => worklist.push(base_class),
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:124:                         // A site with no class behind it means the graph was
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:125:                         // built over a different class set than the one that
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:126:                         // resolved these bases. Nothing is known about that
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:127:                         // edge either.
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:128:                         None => complete = false,
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:129:                     },
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:130:                     ResolvedBase::Form(_) => {}
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:131:                     ResolvedBase::Unknown => complete = false,
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:132:                 }
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:133:             }
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:134:         }
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:135:         Ancestry {
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:136:             classes: order,
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:137:             complete,
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:138:         }
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:139:     }
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:140: 
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:141:     /// [`Self::ancestry`] without the completeness flag, for callers whose
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:142:     /// question is answered by FINDING a class rather than by failing to.
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:143:     #[must_use]
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:144:     pub fn ancestors(&self, class: &'a ClassInfo) -> Vec<&'a ClassInfo> {
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:145:         self.ancestry(class).classes
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:146:     }
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:147: 
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:148:     /// Whether any class in `class`'s chain satisfies `predicate`.
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:149:     fn any_ancestor(&self, class: &'a ClassInfo, predicate: impl Fn(&ClassInfo) -> bool) -> bool {
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:150:         self.ancestors(class).into_iter().any(predicate)
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:151:     }
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:152: 
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:153:     /// Whether `class` is a `TypedDict` — declared directly, or through a base
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:154:     /// this module defines.
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:155:     #[must_use]
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:156:     pub fn is_typed_dict(&self, class: &'a ClassInfo) -> bool {
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:157:         self.any_ancestor(class, |ancestor| ancestor.is_typed_dict)
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:158:     }
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:159: 
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:160:     /// Whether `class` — or any `TypedDict` in its chain — was declared with
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:161:     /// PEP 728's `extra_items=` keyword, which makes unknown keys legal.
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:162:     ///
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:163:     /// Reading the keyword's own name is not a spelling test on a type: a
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:164:     /// class-definition keyword is fixed syntax at the definition site, needs
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:165:     /// no import, and cannot be aliased or rebound.
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:166:     #[must_use]
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:167:     pub fn has_extra_items(&self, class: &'a ClassInfo) -> bool {
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:168:         self.any_ancestor(class, |ancestor| {
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:169:             ancestor.class_keywords.iter().any(|kw| kw == "extra_items")
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:170:         })
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:171:     }
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:172: 
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:173:     /// Every class in this module that is a `TypedDict`, directly or through
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:174:     /// its bases, in declaration order.
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:175:     #[must_use]
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:176:     pub fn typed_dicts(&self) -> Vec<&'a ClassInfo> {
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:177:         self.classes
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:178:             .iter()
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:179:             .filter(|class| self.is_typed_dict(class))
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:180:             .collect()
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:181:     }
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:182: }
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:183: 
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:184: /// The names of every class in `classes` that is a `TypedDict`, directly or
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:185: /// through its bases.
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:186: ///
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:187: /// A convenience for consumers that only hold the class slice and only need
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:188: /// names — the returned names are for lookup by consumers that are themselves
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:189: /// still name-keyed, and carry the collision that implies. Prefer
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:190: /// [`ClassGraph::typed_dicts`], which yields the class definitions.
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/scope/class_graph.rs:191: #[must_use]

crates/basilisk-resolver/src/visitor/annotations.rs:144: /// - `*Ts` — starred name (type-variable-tuple unpack)
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/visitor/annotations.rs:145: ///

crates/basilisk-resolver/src/visitor/annotations.rs:148: /// are accepted here, with `Unpack` recognised by RESOLVING the subscript head

crates/basilisk-resolver/src/visitor/annotations.rs:151: pub(super) fn is_unbounded_component(bindings: &BindingTable, expr: &Expr) -> bool {
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/visitor/annotations.rs:152:     let Some(unpacked) = unpacked_operand(bindings, expr) else {
crates/basilisk-resolver/src/visitor/annotations.rs:153:         return false;

crates/basilisk-resolver/src/visitor/typevar.rs:220:         // `local_value_binding` resolves the name at its own offset, follows
